Pre-Heating Thermocycler to 65°C
Pre-Cooling Temperature Module to 4°C
Setting Temperature Module temperature to 4.0 °C (rounded off to nearest integer)
Setting Thermocycler well block temperature to 65.0 °C
Setting Thermocycler lid temperature to 103.0 °C
Opening Thermocycler lid
Waiting for Temperature Module to reach temperature 4.0 °C (rounded off to nearest integer)
Please place your samples and reagents on the
temperature module.
